97	  rect.y = oy - rect.h;

109	function createRooms(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, ox: number, oy: number): Rect[] {

112	  if (!solidAt(st, ox, py) || py === oy) return [];
113	  const main = findRoom(st, ox, py);
114	  const above = findRoom(st, main.x + (main.w >> 1), main.y + 1);
115	  const below = findRoom(st, main.x + (main.w >> 1), main.y + main.h + 10);
116	  below.y = main.y + main.h - 1;
117	  const pctAbove = solidPct(st, above);
118	  const pctBelow = solidPct(st, below);
119	  main.y += 3; above.y += 3; below.y += 3;
120	  const rooms: Rect[] = [];
121	  if (rng.next() > pctAbove + 0.2) rooms.push(above);
122	  rooms.push(main);
123	  if (rng.next() > pctBelow + 0.2) rooms.push(below);
124	  rooms.sort((a, b) => a.y - b.y);
125	  return rooms;

196	    }
197	  }
198	  if (STATUES_WITH_TRAPS.has(idx)) placeStatueTrap(st, x, y);
199	  return true;
200	}

202	// ---------- 画(RandHousePicture cs:35336 / Desert cs:35286) ----------
203	const PIC_240 = [26, 28, 20, 21, 22, 24, 25, 33, 34, 35, 76, 77, 78, 79, 82, 96, 98, 100];
204	const PIC_245 = [0, 3, 5, 6, 14, -1, 19, 20, 21]; // -1 → 15+rng(4)
205	const PIC_246 = [1, 30, 31, 32, 33, 34, 35, 36];
206	function randHousePicture(rng: RNG): [string, number] {
207	  let n = rng.int(0, 3);
208	  if (n >= 3 && rng.int(0, 1) !== 0) n = rng.int(0, 2);
209	  if (n <= 1) return ['v_240_wall_hangings_3x3', PIC_240[rng.int(0, 17)]];
210	  if (n === 2) {
211	    let s = rng.int(0, 8);
212	    if (s === 0 && rng.int(0, 2) !== 0) s = rng.int(0, 8);
213	    const style = s === 5 ? 15 + rng.int(0, 3) : PIC_245[s];
214	    return ['v_245_paintings_2x3', style];
215	  }
216	  return ['v_246_paintings_3x2', PIC_246[rng.int(0, 7)]];
217	}
218	function randHousePictureDesert(rng: RNG): [string, number] {
219	  const n = rng.int(0, 3);
220	  if (n <= 1) return ['v_240_wall_hangings_3x3', [63, 64, 65, 66, 67, 68][rng.int(0, 5)]];
221	  if (n === 2) return ['v_245_paintings_2x3', 7 + rng.int(0, 1)];
222	  return ['v_242_paintings_6x4', 37 + rng.int(0, 5)];
223	}

225	// ---------- Blotches 相干噪声(Modifiers.Blotches 近似:2 格团簇值噪声) ----------
226	function makeBlotch(seed: number): (x: number, y: number) => number {
227	  const hash = (x: number, y: number) => {
228	    let h = (x * 374761393 + y * 668265263 + seed * 1442695041) | 0;
229	    h = Math.imul(h ^ (h >>> 13), 1274126177);
230	    return ((h ^ (h >>> 16)) >>> 0) / 4294967295;
231	  };
232	  return (x: number, y: number) => {
233	    const gx = Math.floor(x / 2), gy = Math.floor(y / 2);
234	    const fx = x / 2 - gx, fy = y / 2 - gy;
235	    const sx = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx), sy = fy * fy * (3 - 2 * fy);
236	    const a = hash(gx, gy), b = hash(gx + 1, gy), c = hash(gx, gy + 1), d = hash(gx + 1, gy + 1);
237	    return a + (b - a) * sx + (c - a) * sy + (a - b - c + d) * sx * sy;
238	  };
239	}

241	// ---------- AgeRoom 七主题蚀变 ----------
242	/** PlaceTight 冰系钟乳石(PlaceUncheckedStalactite 冰分支:上方∈{147,161,163,164,200}) */
243	function placeStalactiteIce(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number): void {
244	  if (!solidAt(st, x, y - 1) || activeAt(st, x, y) || activeAt(st, x, y + 1)) return;
245	  const above = st.type[st.idx(x, y - 1)];
246	  if (above !== SNOW147 && above !== ICE161
247	    && above !== TILE_BY_KEY['v_163_corrupt_ice_block']! && above !== sheetTile(164)
248	    && above !== TILE_BY_KEY['v_200_crimson_ice_block']!) return;
249	  const preferSmall = rng.int(0, 1) === 0;
250	  const variation = rng.int(0, 2);
251	  // 冰族专属列（GetDesiredStalagtiteStyle :38800-38808 重定型）：147/161→0、
252	  // 163→594、164→540、200→648——曾全写 fx0 冰柱列（163/164/200 错列）
253	  const base = above === SNOW147 || above === ICE161 ? 0
254	    : above === TILE_BY_KEY['v_163_corrupt_ice_block']! ? 594
255	    : above === sheetTile(164) ? 540 : 648;
256	  if (preferSmall) {
257	    st.setTileSilent(x, y, STALACTITE, base + variation * 18, 72);
258	  } else {
259	    st.setTileSilent(x, y, STALACTITE, base + variation * 18, 0);
260	    st.setTileSilent(x, y + 1, STALACTITE, base + variation * 18, 18);
261	  }
262	}
263	/** 花岗岩/大理石/沙漠钟乳石:原版 PlaceUncheckedStalactite 的冰/石双名单不含
264	 *  368/367/397 → 恒 no-op(原版语义,cs:38378 名单外静默返回) */
265	function placeStalactiteNoop(): void { /* 原版名单外无操作 */ }
266	
267	function ageRoom(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, r: Rect, theme: Theme, def: ThemeDef, seed: number): void {
268	  const blob = makeBlotch(seed);
269	  const wallGate = (x: number, y: number, dither: number, blobP: number) =>
270	    rng.next() < dither && blob(x, y) < blobP;
271	  const PAINTINGS = new Set([sheetTile(240), sheetTile(241), sheetTile(242),
272	    TILE_BY_KEY['v_245_paintings_2x3']!, TILE_BY_KEY['v_246_paintings_3x2']!]);
273	
274	  if (theme === 'wood') {
275	    // 蛛网(WoodHouseBuilder.AgeRoom):W*H/16 次 2×2 斑点;Blotches(2,2) 恒过
276	    const count = Math.floor(r.w * r.h / 16);
277	    for (let c = 0; c < count; c++) {
278	      const x = rng.int(1, r.w - 2) + r.x;
279	      const y = rng.int(1, r.h - 2) + r.y;
280	      for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
281	        for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
282	          if (activeAt(st, x + dx, y + dy)) continue;
283	          if (rng.next() < 0.5) st.setTileSilent(x + dx, y + dy, COBWEB, 0, 0);
284	        }
285	      }
286	    }
287	    // 墙侵蚀:OnlyWalls(27);地下清墙/地表换土墙 2
288	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
289	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
290	        if (st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.wall) continue;
291	        if (PAINTINGS.has(st.type[st.idx(x, y)])) continue;
292	        if (rng.next() < 0.85 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) {
293	          st.setWallSilent(x, y, y > gs.worldSurface ? 0 : 2);
294	        }
295	      }
296	    }
297	    return;
298	  }
299	  if (theme === 'ice') {
300	    // 321 →(0.6+团簇)→ 161 →(0.8)→ 147
301	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
302	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
303	        if (st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.tile || !activeAt(st, x, y)) continue;
304	        if (rng.next() < 0.6 && blob(x, y) < 0.6) {
305	          st.setTileSilent(x, y, ICE161);
306	          if (rng.next() < 0.8) st.setTileSilent(x, y, SNOW147);
307	        }
308	      }
309	    }
310	    // 161 行垂冰钟乳石(顶行向内/底行向下)
311	    for (const rowY of [r.y, r.y + r.h - 1]) {
312	      for (let x = r.x + 1; x < r.x + r.w - 1; x++) {
313	        if (activeAt(st, x, rowY) && st.type[st.idx(x, rowY)] === ICE161 && rng.next() < 0.5) {
314	          placeStalactiteIce(st, rng, x, rowY + 1);
315	        }
316	      }
317	    }
318	    // 墙侵蚀(无 OnlyWalls):地下清墙/地表换墙 40;跳过画
319	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
320	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
321	        if (PAINTINGS.has(st.type[st.idx(x, y)])) continue;
322	        if (rng.next() < 0.85 && blob(x, y) < 0.8) {
323	          st.setWallSilent(x, y, y > gs.worldSurface ? 0 : 40);
324	        }
325	      }
326	    }
327	    return;
328	  }
329	  if (theme === 'jungle') {
330	    // 158 →(0.6+团簇)→ 60 →(0.8)→ 59
331	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
332	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
333	        if (st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.tile || !activeAt(st, x, y)) continue;
334	        if (rng.next() < 0.6 && blob(x, y) < 0.6) {
335	          st.setTileSilent(x, y, JUNGLE60);
336	          if (rng.next() < 0.8) st.setTileSilent(x, y, MUD59);
337	        }
338	      }
339	    }
340	    // 60 行垂藤(ActionVines(3, room.Height, 62):长 3..H,遇实心止)
341	    for (const rowY of [r.y, r.y + r.h - 1]) {
342	      for (let x = r.x + 1; x < r.x + r.w - 1; x++) {
343	        if (activeAt(st, x, rowY) && st.type[st.idx(x, rowY)] === JUNGLE60 && rng.next() < 0.5) {
344	          const len = rng.int(3, r.h);
345	          for (let i = 0; i < len && !activeAt(st, x, rowY + 1 + i); i++) {
346	            st.setTileSilent(x, rowY + 1 + i, VINE62, 0, 0);
347	          }
348	        }
349	      }
350	    }
351	    // 墙 → 64(无 OnlyWalls)
352	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
353	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
354	        if (rng.next() < 0.85 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) st.setWallSilent(x, y, 64);
355	      }
356	    }
357	    return;
358	  }
359	  if (theme === 'mushroom') {
360	    // 190 →(0.7+团簇0.5)→ 70
361	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
362	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
363	        if (st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.tile || !activeAt(st, x, y)) continue;
364	        if (rng.next() < 0.7 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) st.setTileSilent(x, y, MUSH70);
365	      }
366	    }
367	    // 70 行上方发光菇 71(Offset(0,-1),IsEmpty)
368	    for (const rowY of [r.y, r.y + r.h - 1]) {
369	      for (let x = r.x + 1; x < r.x + r.w - 1; x++) {
370	        if (activeAt(st, x, rowY) && st.type[st.idx(x, rowY)] === MUSH70 && rng.next() < 0.6) {
371	          if (!activeAt(st, x, rowY - 1)) st.setTileSilent(x, rowY - 1, SHROOM71, 0, 0);
372	        }
373	      }
374	    }
375	    // 墙清空(无 OnlyWalls)
376	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
377	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
378	        if (rng.next() < 0.85 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) st.setWallSilent(x, y, 0);
379	      }
380	    }
381	    return;
382	  }
383	  if (theme === 'granite' || theme === 'marble') {
384	    // 369→368 / 357→367(0.6+团簇);钟乳石 = 原版名单外 no-op;墙 → 180/178
385	    const to = theme === 'granite' ? GRANITE368 : MARBLE367;
386	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
387	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
388	        if (st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.tile || !activeAt(st, x, y)) continue;
389	        if (rng.next() < 0.6 && blob(x, y) < 0.6) st.setTileSilent(x, y, to);
390	      }
391	    }
392	    for (const rowY of [r.y, r.y + r.h - 1]) {
393	      for (let x = r.x + 1; x < r.x + r.w - 1; x++) {
394	        const t = st.type[st.idx(x, rowY)];
395	        if (activeAt(st, x, rowY) && t === to && rng.next() < 0.8) placeStalactiteNoop();
396	      }
397	    }
398	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
399	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
400	        if (rng.next() < 0.85 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) st.setWallSilent(x, y, theme === 'granite' ? 180 : 178);
401	      }
402	    }
403	    return;
404	  }
405	  // desert:396 →(0.8+团簇0.2)→ 397;钟乳石名单外 no-op;OnlyWalls(187) → 墙 216
406	  for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
407	    for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
408	      if (st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.tile || !activeAt(st, x, y)) continue;
409	      if (rng.next() < 0.8 && blob(x, y) < 0.2 && rng.next() < 0.5) st.setTileSilent(x, y, HARDSAND397);
410	    }
411	  }
412	  for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
413	    for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
414	      if (st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.wall) continue;
415	      if (rng.next() < 0.8 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) st.setWallSilent(x, y, 216);
416	    }
417	  }
418	}

420	// ---------- FillRooms(画/烛台交替 + 装饰循环) ----------
421	function fillRooms(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, r: Rect, def: ThemeDef, theme: Theme): void {
422	  // 家具七选(HouseBuilder.FillRooms choices;沙漠 Tables2 → tile 469)
423	  const choices: Array<[string, number]> = [
424	    [def.tables2 ? 'v_469_tables_group_2' : 'table', def.table],
425	    ['anvil', 0],
426	    ['workbench', def.workbench],
427	    ['v_86_loom', 0],
428	    ['piano', def.piano],
429	    ['keg', 0],
430	    ['bookcase', def.bookcase],
431	  ];
432	  // 画/烛台交替:switch(i + parity%2) 仅 0/1 两档有内容(i≥2 落空,原版同款)
433	  const n = Math.floor(r.w / 8);
434	  const step = Math.floor(r.w / (n + 1));
435	  const parity = rng.int(0, 1);
436	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
437	    const x = (i + 1) * step + r.x;
438	    if ((i + (parity % 2)) % 2 === 0) {
439	      // 画:Y + min(H/2, H-5)
440	      const y = r.y + Math.min(Math.floor(r.h / 2), r.h - 5);
441	      const [key, style] = theme === 'desert' ? randHousePictureDesert(rng) : randHousePicture(rng);
442	      placeFurniture(st, key, x, y, style);
443	    } else {
444	      // 烛台:顶行悬挂(原版 +54 点亮帧——本引擎烛台恒点亮,帧差不建模)
445	      const y = r.y + 1;
446	      placeFurniture(st, 'candelabra', x, y, rng.int(0, 5), 'top');
447	    }
448	  }
449	  // 装饰循环:W/8+3 件
450	  const decoCount = Math.floor(r.w / 8) + 3;
451	  for (let k = 0; k < decoCount; k++) {
452	    const x = rng.int(0, r.w - 4) + 1 + r.x;
453	    const y = r.y + r.h - 2;
454	    switch (rng.int(0, 3)) {
455	      case 0: // 小堆 185(PlaceSmallPile size1:style*18)
456	        if (!activeAt(st, x, y)) st.setTileSilent(x, y, SMALL_PILE, rng.int(31, 33) * 18, 0);
457	        break;
458	      case 1: // 骨堆 186
459	        placeFurniture(st, 'v_186_decos_3x2', x, y, rng.int(22, 25));
460	        break;
461	      case 2: // 雕像(statueList[Next(2, len)],陷阱雕像照接)
462	        placeStatue(st, x, y, rng.int(2, STATUE_LIST.length - 1));
463	        break;
464	      case 3: {
465	        const [key, style] = choices[rng.int(0, choices.length - 1)];
466	        placeFurniture(st, key, x, y, style);
467	        break;
468	      }
469	    }
470	  }
471	}
472	
473	// ---------- 楼梯 / 门 / 平台 / 梁 ----------
474	/** CreateStairsList + PlaceStairs:相邻房间对角平台链 + 顶步 4×1 平台 + 斜坡 */
475	function placeStairs(st: TileStore, rooms: Rect[], def: ThemeDef): void {
476	  for (let i = 1; i < rooms.length; i++) {
477	    const cur = rooms[i], prev = rooms[i - 1];
478	    const leftGap = prev.x - cur.x;
479	    const rightGap = cur.x + cur.w - (prev.x + prev.w);
480	    let from: { x: number; y: number };
481	    if (leftGap > rightGap) from = { x: cur.x + cur.w - 1, y: cur.y + 1 };
482	    else from = { x: cur.x, y: cur.y + 1 };
483	    const steps = (cur.y + cur.h - 1) - from.y;
484	    const dir = leftGap > rightGap ? -1 : 1;
485	    for (let s = 0; s < steps; s++) {
486	      const sx = from.x + dir * (s + 1), sy = from.y + s;
487	      if (!st.inBounds(sx, sy)) continue;
488	      st.setTileSilent(sx, sy, PLATFORM, 0, 0);
489	      st.slope[st.idx(sx, sy)] = dir === 1 ? 1 : 2;
490	    }
491	    // 顶步上方 4×1 平台(清空+补墙+平台)
492	    const px = from.x + (dir === 1 ? 1 : -4);
493	    for (let dx = 0; dx < 4; dx++) {
494	      if (!st.inBounds(px + dx, from.y - 1)) continue;
495	      st.setTileSilent(px + dx, from.y - 1, PLATFORM, 0, 0);
496	      st.setWallSilent(px + dx, from.y - 1, def.wall);
497	    }
498	  }
499	}
500	
501	/** FindSideExit(Up 搜索 + AreaOr(4,3) 任一非实心) */
502	function findSideExit(st: TileStore, wallX: number, wallY: number, wallH: number, isLeft: boolean): number | null {
503	  const startX = wallX + (isLeft ? -4 : 0);
504	  for (let k = 0; k < wallH - 3; k++) {
505	    const y = wallY + wallH - 3 - k;
506	    let any = false;
507	    for (let dx = 0; dx < 4 && !any; dx++) {
508	      for (let dy = 0; dy < 3 && !any; dy++) {
509	        if (!solidAt(st, startX + dx, y + dy)) any = true;
510	      }
511	    }
512	    if (any) return y;
513	  }
514	  return null;
515	}
516	
517	function placeDoors(st: TileStore, rooms: Rect[], def: ThemeDef): void {
518	  for (const r of rooms) {
519	    // 墙列矩形 (X, Y+1, 1, H-2)
520	    // 门禁（原版 PlaceTile(x, exitY)→PlaceDoor(j+1)，WorldGen.cs:31946）：mount
521	    // （exitY-1 实心）+ 地板（exitY+3 实心）缺一即不放门只留门洞——HouseBuilder
522	    // 的 forced=true 仅绕过首道空格门，门分支的锚点条件仍生效
523	    const tryDoor = (x: number, exitY: number) => {
524	      for (let dy = 0; dy < 3; dy++) st.setTileSilent(x, exitY + dy, 0);
525	      if (solidAt(st, x, exitY - 1) && solidAt(st, x, exitY + 3)) {
526	        placeDoorClosed(st, x, exitY, def.door);
527	      }
528	    };
529	    const exitR = findSideExit(st, r.x + r.w - 1, r.y + 1, r.h - 2, false);
530	    if (exitR !== null) tryDoor(r.x + r.w - 1, exitR);
531	    const exitL = findSideExit(st, r.x, r.y + 1, r.h - 2, true);
532	    if (exitL !== null) tryDoor(r.x, exitL);
533	  }
534	}
535	
536	/** FindVerticalExit(Left 搜索 + AreaOr(3,5) 任一非实心) */
537	function findVerticalExit(st: TileStore, rectX: number, rectY: number, rectW: number, isUp: boolean): number | null {
538	  const startX = rectX + rectW - 3;
539	  const startY = rectY + (isUp ? -5 : 0);
540	  for (let k = 0; k < rectW - 3; k++) {
541	    const x = startX - k;
542	    let any = false;
543	    for (let dx = 0; dx < 3 && !any; dx++) {
544	      for (let dy = 0; dy < 5 && !any; dy++) {
545	        if (!solidAt(st, x + dx, startY + dy)) any = true;
546	      }
547	    }
548	    if (any) return x;
549	  }
550	  return null;
551	}
552	
553	function placePlatforms(st: TileStore, rooms: Rect[]): void {
554	  const top = rooms[0], bot = rooms[rooms.length - 1];
555	  // 顶层顶壁(isUp:候选自壁上 5 格起)
556	  const exitT = findVerticalExit(st, top.x + 2, top.y, top.w - 4, true);
557	  if (exitT !== null) {
558	    for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) st.setTileSilent(exitT + dx, top.y, PLATFORM, 0, 0);
559	  }
560	  if (rooms.length > 1) {
561	    const exitB = findVerticalExit(st, bot.x + 2, bot.y + bot.h - 1, bot.w - 4, false);
562	    if (exitB !== null) {
563	      for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) st.setTileSilent(exitB + dx, bot.y + bot.h - 1, PLATFORM, 0, 0);
564	    }
565	  }
566	}
567	
568	/** CreateSupportBeamList + PlaceSupportBeams:6→4 步长梁列,落下一房顶/实心 */
569	function placeSupportBeams(st: TileStore, rooms: Rect[], def: ThemeDef): void {
570	  const minX = Math.min(...rooms.map((r) => r.x));
571	  const maxX = Math.max(...rooms.map((r) => r.x + r.w)) - 1;
572	  let step = 6;
573	  while (step > 4 && (maxX - minX) % step !== 0) step--;
574	  for (let x = minX; x <= maxX; x += step) {
575	    for (let ri = 0; ri < rooms.length; ri++) {
576	      const r = rooms[ri];
577	      if (x < r.x || x >= r.x + r.w) continue;
578	      const bottom = r.y + r.h;
579	      let limit = 50;
580	      for (let rj = ri + 1; rj < rooms.length; rj++) {
581	        const r2 = rooms[rj];
582	        if (x >= r2.x && x < r2.x + r2.w) limit = Math.min(limit, r2.y - bottom);
583	      }
584	      if (limit <= 0) continue;
585	      let found = -1;
586	      if (limit < 50) found = bottom + limit; // 原版:num7<50 视为命中下一房顶
587	      else {
588	        for (let y = bottom; y < bottom + limit; y++) {
589	          if (solidAt(st, x, y)) { found = y; break; }
590	        }
591	      }
592	      if (found < 0) continue;
593	      const height = found - bottom;
594	      if (height > 1 && st.type[st.idx(x, bottom - 1)] !== PLATFORM) {
595	        for (let y = bottom; y < found; y++) st.setTileSilent(x, y, def.beam, 0, 0);
596	      }
597	    }
598	  }
599	}
600	
601	// ---------- 宝箱四级联(PlaceChests) ----------
602	function placeChests(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink, rooms: Rect[], def: ThemeDef, theme: Theme): void {
603	  const tryAt = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
604	    const underground = y > gs.worldSurface;
605	    // 冰屋：显式 style 11 直入冰箱门（cs:36107 第一析取）→ 主件池 + 1/20 提炼机 +
606	    // 1/50 鱼覆盖块自动生效（旧实现显式 loot 绕过覆盖——审计三.4 修复）
607	    return placeBuriedChest(st, rng, gs, chests, x, y, {
608	      style: underground ? def.chestStyle : 0,
609	      tile: underground && def.chestTile2 ? CHEST2 : undefined,
610	      flags: theme === 'ice' ? { ice: true } : (!underground ? { wooden: true } : {}),
611	    });
612	  };
613	  // 级联 1:各房底行随机 10 次
614	  for (const r of rooms) {
615	    const floorY = r.y + r.h - 1;
616	    let ok = false;
617	    for (let t = 0; t < 10 && !ok; t++) ok = tryAt(rng.int(2, r.w - 3) + r.x, floorY);
618	    if (ok) return;
619	    for (let x = r.x + 2; x <= r.x + r.w - 2 && !ok; x++) ok = tryAt(x, floorY);
620	    if (ok) return;
621	  }
622	  // 级联 2:各房顶行(Y-1)
623	  for (const r of rooms) {
624	    const ceilY = r.y - 1;
625	    let ok = false;
626	    for (let t = 0; t < 10 && !ok; t++) ok = tryAt(rng.int(2, r.w - 3) + r.x, ceilY);
627	    if (ok) return;
628	    for (let x = r.x + 2; x <= r.x + r.w - 2 && !ok; x++) ok = tryAt(x, ceilY);
629	    if (ok) return;
630	  }
631	  // 级联 3:房外 ±30 随机 1000 次
632	  for (let t = 0; t < 1000; t++) {
633	    if (tryAt(rng.int(rooms[0].x - 30, rooms[0].x + 29), rng.int(rooms[0].y - 30, rooms[0].y + 29))) return;
634	  }
635	}
636	
637	// ---------- pass 主体 ----------
638	/** 地下小屋 pass(UndergroundHousesAndBuriedChests 的 CaveHouse 段,cs:17153-17178
639	 *  + 沙漠追加段 cs:17193-17219) */
640	export function runCaveHousesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {
641	  const { w, h } = st;
642	  const count = Math.round(rng.int(35, 40) * (w * h) / 5040000);
643	  // 第 4 个 WorldGenRange：AdditionalDesertHouseCount（2-2 × WorldArea，
644	  // Configuration.json）——GetRandom 恒消费一次 Next（cs:17088）
645	  const desertHouses = rng.int(2, 2);
646	  let bastCount = 0;        // GenVars.extraBastStatueCount(≤2)
647	  let extractCount = 0;     // HouseBuilderContext.ExtractinatorCount
648	  const extractMax = rng.int(2, 4);
649	  let sharpCount = 0;       // context.SharpenerCount
650	  const sharpMax = rng.int(2, 4);
651	
652	  /** 单座小屋放置（HouseBuilder.Place 原序），返回是否成功 */
653	  const placeHouse = (ox: number, oy: number, oceanGate: boolean): boolean => {
654	    if (oceanGate && (ox < gs.beachDistance || ox > w - gs.beachDistance)) return false; // oceanDepths 回避
655	    const rooms = createRooms(st, rng, ox, oy);
656	    if (!rooms.length) return false;
657	    // 位置合法(InWorld 10 边距 / 底部 < h-220)+ 房间互不重叠
658	    let ok = true;
659	    for (const r of rooms) {
660	      if (r.x < 10 || r.y < 10 || r.x + r.w > w - 10 || r.y + r.h > h - 220) ok = false;
661	    }
662	    for (let a = 0; a < rooms.length && ok; a++) {
663	      for (let b = a + 1; b < rooms.length && ok; b++) {
664	        const A = rooms[a], B = rooms[b];
665	        if (A.x < B.x + B.w && B.x < A.x + A.w && A.y < B.y + B.h && B.y < A.y + A.h) ok = false;
666	      }
667	    }
668	    if (!ok) return false;
669	    const theme = getHouseType(st, rooms);
670	    const def = THEMES[theme];
671	    if (def.tile < 0 || def.beam < 0) return false;
672	    // 岩浆回避(granite 主题豁免,HouseUtils.AreRoomsValid)
673	    if (theme !== 'granite') {
674	      for (const r of rooms) {
675	        for (let x = r.x - 2; x < r.x + r.w + 2; x++) {
676	          for (let y = r.y - 2; y < r.y + r.h + 2; y++) {
677	            if (st.inBounds(x, y) && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] > 0 && st.liquidType[st.idx(x, y)] === 2) ok = false;
678	          }
679	        }
680	      }
681	      if (!ok) return false;
682	    }
683	
684	    // ---- HouseBuilder.Place 原序 ----
685	    // 1) PlaceEmptyRooms:外壳 + 内腔墙
686	    for (const r of rooms) {
687	      for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
688	        for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
689	          st.setTileSilent(x, y, def.tile);
690	          st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] = 0;
691	          st.liquidType[st.idx(x, y)] = 0;
692	        }
693	      }
694	      for (let x = r.x + 1; x < r.x + r.w - 1; x++) {
695	        for (let y = r.y + 1; y < r.y + r.h - 1; y++) {
696	          st.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
697	          st.setWallSilent(x, y, def.wall);
698	        }
699	      }
700	    }
701	    // 2-4) 楼梯 / 门 / 平台
702	    placeStairs(st, rooms, def);
703	    placeDoors(st, rooms, def);
704	    placePlatforms(st, rooms);
705	    // 5) 支撑梁
706	    placeSupportBeams(st, rooms, def);
707	    // 6) 沙漠 Bast 石碑(优先件,≤2)
708	    if (theme === 'desert' && bastCount < 2) {
709	      const r = rooms[0];
710	      if (placeFurniture(st, 'v_506_bast_statue', r.x + 1 + rng.int(0, Math.max(1, r.w - 3)), r.y + r.h - 2)) bastCount++;
711	    }
712	    // 7) FillRooms:画/烛台 + 装饰(每房)
713	    for (const r of rooms) fillRooms(st, rng, r, def, theme);
714	    // 8) AgeRoom:七主题蚀变
715	    const seed = rng.int(0, 1 << 30);
716	    for (const r of rooms) ageRoom(st, rng, gs, r, theme, def, seed);
717	    // 9) 宝箱(ChestChance=1.0,四级联)
718	    placeChests(st, rng, gs, chests, rooms, def, theme);
719	    // 10) 主题专属件:丛林磨刀站 / 沙漠提炼机
720	    if (theme === 'jungle' && sharpCount < sharpMax) {
721	      const r = rooms[0];
722	      if (placeFurniture(st, 'v_377_sharpening_station', r.x + 1 + rng.int(0, Math.max(1, r.w - 3)), r.y + r.h - 2)) sharpCount++;
723	    }
724	    if (theme === 'desert' && extractCount < extractMax) {
725	      const r = rooms[rooms.length - 1];
726	      if (placeFurniture(st, 'v_219_extractinator', r.x + 1 + rng.int(0, Math.max(1, r.w - 3)), r.y + r.h - 2)) extractCount++;
727	    }
728	    return true;
729	  };
730	
731	  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {
732	    const ox = rng.int(80, w - 81);
733	    const oy = rng.int(Math.ceil(gs.worldSurfaceHigh + 20), h - 231);
734	    placeHouse(ox, oy, true);
735	  }
736	
737	  // ---- 沙漠追加小屋（cs:17193-17219）：UndergroundDesertHiveLocation 内追加
738	  //  AdditionalDesertHouseCount 座（RandomRectanglePoint），失败预算 10000 重试同下标
739	  const ud = gs.undergroundDesert;
740	  if (ud && ud.x1 > ud.x0 && ud.y1 > ud.y0) {
741	    let ry = ud.y0;
742	    let rh = ud.y1 - ud.y0;
743	    if (ry < gs.worldSurface + 26) {          // cs:17200-17205：上缘压到 worldSurface+26 下
744	      const cut = Math.floor(gs.worldSurface) + 26 - ry;
745	      ry += cut;
746	      rh -= cut;
747	    }
748	    if (rh > 0) {
749	      let budget = 10000;
750	      for (let l = 0; l < desertHouses;) {
751	        if (budget <= 0) break;
752	        const hx = rng.int(ud.x0, ud.x1);      // RandomRectanglePoint: Next(X, X+Width)
753	        const hy = rng.int(ry, ry + rh);
754	        if (!placeHouse(hx, hy, false)) { budget--; continue; }
755	        l++;
756	      }
757	    }
758	  }
759	}
760	

```

	public class Blotches : GenAction
	{
		private int _minX;

		private int _minY;

		private int _maxX;

		private int _maxY;

		private double _chance;

		public Blotches(int scale = 2, double chance = 0.3)
		{
			_minX = scale;
			_minY = scale;
			_maxX = scale;
			_maxY = scale;
			_chance = chance;
		}

		public Blotches(int xScale, int yScale, double chance = 0.3)
		{
			_minX = xScale;
			_maxX = xScale;
			_minY = yScale;
			_maxY = yScale;
			_chance = chance;
		}

		public Blotches(int leftScale, int upScale, int rightScale, int downScale, double chance = 0.3)
		{
			_minX = leftScale;
			_maxX = rightScale;
			_minY = upScale;
			_maxY = downScale;
			_chance = chance;
		}

		public override bool Apply(Point origin, int x, int y, params object[] args)
		{
			GenBase._random.NextDouble();

So: for each point, first discard one NextDouble(), then roll NextDouble() < chance. If blotch forms, apply to random rectangle [num..num2]×[num3..num4] where num∈[1-minX, 0], num2∈[0, maxX-1], etc. Note `Next(0, _maxX)` gives 0..maxX-1. And note the rectangle is relative offsets applied to (x+i, y+j) — so it overflows outside the room rect.

Per C1, must be `rng.next() >= 0.5`.
   - Also Blotches(2,2): minX=maxX=minY=maxY=2, chance=0.3 default. So per cell: discard one NextDouble, then if NextDouble < 0.3 → blotch: offsets num=Next(1-2,1)=Next(-1,1) → -1 or 0; num2=Next(0,2) → 0 or 1; same for Y. Apply chain remainder (IsEmpty, SetTile 51) to each cell in [x-1..x+1]×[y-1..y+1]... wait num..num2 range: i from num(-1..0) to num2(0..1) → offsets in {-1,0}..{0,1}. So the blotch rect covers offsets from -1..1 in each axis, up to 3×3. Actually since num ∈ {-1, 0} and num2 ∈ {0, 1}, the union across draws is -1..1. Per single draw it's [num..num2] which could be e.g. [-1..0], [-1..1], [0..0], [0..1].
   - IsEmpty then applies to each of those target cells; SetTile(51) on non-empty fail.

2. Full room rect: chain Dither(0.85) [15% pass], Blotches() [default 2, chance 0.3], OnlyWalls(WallType=27), SkipTiles(SkipTilesDuringWallAging), then Y>worldSurface ? ClearWall(frameNeighbors:true) : PlaceWall(2).
   - Current code: `rng.next() < 0.85 && blob(x,y) < 0.5` — wrong twice (Dither inverted, Blotches approximated).

   - So: 321→161 happens at 40% pass × blotch(0.6), and then for cells where the first part passed and set 161, a second Dither(0.8) [20% pass] converts to 147. =====WorldGenRange=====
using Newtonsoft.Json;
using Newtonsoft.Json.Converters;
using Terraria.Utilities;

namespace Terraria.WorldBuilding;

public class WorldGenRange
{
	public enum ScalingMode
	{
		None,
		WorldArea,
		WorldWidth
	}

	public static readonly WorldGenRange Empty = new WorldGenRange(0, 0);

	[JsonProperty("Min")]
	public readonly int Minimum;

	[JsonProperty("Max")]
	public readonly int Maximum;

	[JsonProperty]
	[JsonConverter(typeof(StringEnumConverter))]
	public readonly ScalingMode ScaleWith;

	public int ScaledMinimum => ScaleValue(Minimum);

	public int ScaledMaximum => ScaleValue(Maximum);

	public WorldGenRange(int minimum, int maximum)
	{
		Minimum = minimum;
		Maximum = maximum;
	}

	public int GetRandom(UnifiedRandom random)
	{
		return random.Next(ScaledMinimum, ScaledMaximum + 1);
	}

	private int ScaleValue(int value)
	{
		double num = 1.0;
		switch (ScaleWith)
		{
		case ScalingMode.WorldArea:
			num = (double)(Main.maxTilesX * Main.maxTilesY) / 5040000.0;
			break;
		case ScalingMode.WorldWidth:
			num = (double)Main.maxTilesX / 4200.0;
			break;
		case ScalingMode.None:
			num = 1.0;
			break;
		}
		return (int)(num * (double)value);
	}
}

export class UnifiedRandom {
  private seedArray: Int32Array;
  private inext = 0;

  constructor(seed: number) {
    this.seedArray = new Int32Array(56);
    this.setSeed(seed);
  }

  /** C# SetSeed（UnifiedRandom.cs:25-61）1:1 */
  setSeed(seed: number): void {
    this.seedArray.fill(0);

    const num = seed === -2147483648 ? 2147483647 : Math.abs(seed);
    let num2 = 161803398 - num;
    this.seedArray[55] = num2;
    let num3 = 1;
    for (let j = 1; j < 55; j++) {
      const num4 = (21 * j) % 55;
      this.seedArray[num4] = num3;
      num3 = (num2 - num3) | 0; // C# unchecked int：可能回绕，|0 = ToInt32 同语义
      if (num3 < 0) num3 += 2147483647; // C# += int.MaxValue（不是 2^32！）
      num2 = this.seedArray[num4];
    }
    for (let k = 1; k < 5; k++) {
      for (let l = 1; l < 56; l++) {
        this.seedArray[l] -= this.seedArray[1 + ((l + 30) % 55)];
        if (this.seedArray[l] < 0) this.seedArray[l] += 2147483647;
      }
    }
    this.inext = 0;
  }

  /** C# Sample（:63-65）*/
  private sample(): number {
    return this.internalSample() * 4.656612875245797e-10;
  }

  /** C# InternalSample（:67-86）1:1。inext 为 uint 语义（0..55，无溢出面）*/
  private internalSample(): number {
    let num = this.inext + 1;
    if (num > 55) num = 1;
    let num2 = num + 21;
    if (num2 > 55) num2 -= 55;
    let num3 = this.seedArray[num] - this.seedArray[num2];
    if (num3 === 2147483647) num3--;
    num3 = num3 + ((num3 >> 31) & 0x7fffffff); // >>31 算术移位，JS/C# 一致
    this.seedArray[num] = num3;
    this.inext = num;
    return num3;
  }

  /** C# GetSampleForLargeRange（:96-104）。注意两次 InternalSample 的求值顺序 */
  private getSampleForLargeRange(): number {
    let num = this.internalSample();
    if (this.internalSample() % 2 === 0) num = -num;
    return (num + 2147483646) / 4294967293;
  }

/** Buried Chests pass:L7907 洞穴段(CaveChestCount 35-40 × WorldArea/5040000)。
 *  ★地狱段拆出 runUnderworldChestsPass:本函数曾在 hellPass 之前执行,
 *  地狱箱被后续地狱地形重写 100% 抹除(实际 0 个) */
export function runBuriedChestsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {
  const { w, h } = st;
  const yMin = Math.floor((gs.worldSurfaceHigh + 20 + gs.rockLevel) / 2);
  // 洞穴箱:35-40 × 面积缩放(Configuration.json CaveChestCount ScaleWith:WorldArea;
  // 小世界(4200×1200=5040000)= 1.0 → 35-40;中 ×2.29;大 ×4.0)
  const target = Math.round(rng.int(35, 40) * (w * h) / 5040000);
  let fail = 10000;
  for (let n = 0; n < target && fail > 0; ) {
    const x = rng.int(20, w - 21);
    const y = rng.int(yMin, h - 231);
    const wl = st.inBounds(x, y) ? st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] : -1;
    // cs:17109：wallDungeon[wall] || wall == 87（神庙墙 87 独立门）→ 整体重掷
    if (isDungeonWall(wl) || wl === 87 || !placeBuriedChest(st, rng, gs, chests, x, y)) {
      fail--; continue;
    }
    n++;
  }
}

/** 地狱箱(UnderworldChestCount 10-15 × WorldWidth/4200)。
 *  必须在 hellPass + 地狱屋之后调用(原版 Underworld(cs:13697) < Buried Chests(cs:17066)) */
export function runUnderworldChestsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {
  const { w, h } = st;
  const target = Math.round(rng.int(10, 15) * (w / 4200));
  let fail = 10000;
  for (let n = 0; n < target && fail > 0; ) {
    const x = rng.int(20, w - 21);
    const y = rng.int(h - 200, h - 51);
    if (!placeBuriedChest(st, rng, gs, chests, x, y)) {
      fail--; continue;
    }
    n++;
  }
}

Desert segment verified: 17193-17219. The current implementation matches structurally (budget 10000, retry same index). ✓

Now, the remaining piece for C2: I need the exact Blotches line numbers in Modifiers.cs, and also WorldUtils.Gen/Chain semantics to model "Dither → Blotches → OnlyTiles → ..." faithfully in terms of RNG consumption and application.

Key modeling questions for the rewrite:

1. **How does WorldUtils.Gen iterate?** For `Shapes.Rectangle(w,h)` starting at origin — iterates each point (x from origin.X to origin.X+w-1, y similar) and applies the chain. Order matters for RNG. Let me check Shapes.Rectangle Perform and WorldUtils.Gen.

2. **Blotches behavior**: per point, consumes 2 NextDouble rolls (one discarded, one compared to chance) PLUS 4 Next rolls if blotch forms. Then applies the REST OF THE CHAIN (from the next action onward — actually UnitApply continues the chain at the current action's output) to each cell in the random rect.

Wait — important subtlety. In a Chain, `Blotches.Apply` receives the chain and calls `UnitApply(origin, x+i, y+j, args)` which applies the NEXT action in the chain at the offset coordinates. So when a blotch forms, the subsequent actions (OnlyTiles, SetTileKeepWall, ...) are applied to cells in the random rectangle [x+num..x+num2]×[y+num3..y+num4]. And the return is !flag where flag = any UnitApply failed.

If no blotch (NextDouble >= chance), it applies UnitApply at (x,y) — the chain continues at the original point.

So for e.g. Ice segment 1: chain = Dither(0.6), Blotches(2, 0.6), OnlyTiles(321), SetTileKeepWall(161), Dither(0.8), SetTileKeepWall(147).

For each point (x,y) in room rect:
- Dither(0.6): roll r1; if r1 < 0.6 → Fail (chain stops, no further RNG consumed for this point!). Else continue.
- Blotches(2,0.6): roll r2 (discarded), roll r3; if r3 < 0.6 → blotch: num=Next(1-2,1)=Next(-1,1) ∈ {-1,0}, num2=Next(0,2) ∈ {0,1}, num3∈{-1,0}, num4∈{0,1}; for each (i,j) in [num..num2]×[num3..num4]: apply OnlyTiles(321)→SetTileKeepWall(161)→Dither(0.8)→SetTileKeepWall(147) at (x+i, y+j). 
  - Note: OnlyTiles checks the tile at the OFFSET cell. The room's tile at that offset might be 321 (from PlaceEmptyRooms) or something else (could be outside the room, e.g. terrain).
  - Dither(0.8) inside: roll per offset cell; if roll >= 0.8 → SetTileKeepWall(147).
- else (r3 >= 0.6): apply chain at (x,y): OnlyTiles(321) at (x,y) → if tile==321: SetTileKeepWall(161); then Dither(0.8) roll; if >= 0.8 → SetTileKeepWall(147).

So the entire AgeRoom chain application is pointwise with blotch fan-out. This is very different from the current hash-noise approximation.

RNG consumption per point: 1 (Dither) + 2 (Blotches discard+chance) + [if blotch: 4 Next + per-offset-cell sub-rolls]. Sub-chain: OnlyTiles (no RNG), SetTileKeepWall (no RNG), Dither(0.8) (1 roll per cell where OnlyTiles passed and SetTileKeepWall succeeded).

	{
		private ushort _type;

		public SetTileKeepWall(ushort type, bool setSelfFrames = false, bool setNeighborFrames = true)

			int wallFrameX = GenBase._tiles[x, y].wallFrameX();
			int wallFrameY = GenBase._tiles[x, y].wallFrameY();
			GenBase._tiles[x, y].Clear(~(TileDataType.Wiring | TileDataType.Actuator));
			GenBase._tiles[x, y].type = _type;
			GenBase._tiles[x, y].active(active: true);
			if (wall > 0)

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Key semantics:
- `SetTileKeepWall(type, setSelfFrames)`: saves wall, clears tile (keeping wiring/actuator), sets type, active, restores wall if >0, optional framing. Always succeeds (returns UnitApply). Note: this means it can place tiles into cells that had other tiles (e.g. overwrites 321→161 unconditionally once OnlyTiles passed).
- `SetTile(type, setSelfFrames, ..., clearTile: true)`: clears tile, sets type, active.

Both always continue the chain (no Fail).

Important for the Jungle vine chain: `OnlyTiles(60) → Offset(0,1) → IsEmpty → ActionVines`. Offset applies the REST of the chain at (x, y+1). So ActionVines runs at (x, rowY+1) after IsEmpty check at (x, rowY+1).

For Ice: `Dither() → OnlyTiles(161) → Offset(0,1) → ActionStalagtite` — ActionStalagtite at (x, rowY+1) where rowY cell is 161.

Now let me read ActionStalagtite and ActionVines (in Terraria.GameContent.Generation presumably).
```

	public class Rectangle : GenShape
	{
		private Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Rectangle _area;

		public Rectangle(Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Rectangle area)
		{
			_area = area;
		}

		public Rectangle(int width, int height)
		{
			_area = new Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Rectangle(0, 0, width, height);
		}

		public void SetArea(Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Rectangle area)
		{
			_area = area;
		}

		public override bool Perform(Point origin, GenAction action)
		{
			for (int i = origin.X + _area.Left; i < origin.X + _area.Right; i++)
			{
				for (int j = origin.Y + _area.Top; j < origin.Y + _area.Bottom; j++)
				{
					if (!UnitApply(action, origin, i, j) && _quitOnFail)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
			}
			return true;
		}
	}

Design: implement per-theme AgeRoom with a generic helper that simulates the chain:

```ts
type ChainStep =
  | { k: 'dither'; p: number }          // pass iff next() >= p
  | { k: 'blotches'; minX; maxX; minY; maxY; chance }  // blotch fan-out
  | { k: 'onlyTiles'; types: number[] }
  | { k: 'onlyWalls'; types: number[] }
  | { k: 'skipTiles'; types: number[] }
  | { k: 'isEmpty' }

  | { k: 'stalagtite' }                  // PlaceTight
  | { k: 'vines'; min; max; vine };

function runChain(st, rng, gs, steps: ChainStep[], i: number, x: number, y: number): boolean
```
Recursive: apply step i at (x,y); if it's blotches, fan out with recursion for steps i+1.. at offset cells.

```ts
// ---------- 原版 GenAction 链解释器(Modifiers/Actions 1:1) ----------
// Dither.cs(Modifiers.cs:620-635):参数是【失败率】——NextDouble() >= _failureChance 才 UnitApply。
// Blotches(Modifiers.cs:92-152):每点先丢 1 掷再比 chance;成斑则把后续链应用到
//   随机矩形 [x+Next(1-minX,1) .. x+Next(0,maxX)] × [y+Next(1-minY,1) .. y+Next(0,maxY)]
//   (会外溢房间矩形);未成斑则原点继续。
type ChainStep =
  | { k: 'dither'; p: number }
  | { k: 'blotches'; minX: number; maxX: number; minY: number; maxY: number; chance: number }
  | { k: 'onlyTiles'; types: number[] }
  | { k: 'onlyWalls'; types: number[] }
  | { k: 'skipTiles'; types: number[] }
  | { k: 'isEmpty' }
  | { k: 'offset'; dx: number; dy: number }
  | { k: 'setTileKeepWall'; tile: number }
  | { k: 'setTile'; tile: number }
  | { k: 'clearTile' }
  | { k: 'clearWall' }
  | { k: 'placeWall'; wall: number }
  | { k: 'stalagtite' }
  | { k: 'vines'; min: number; max: number; vine: number };

/** Apply(actions[i], x, y) 1:1(含 Blotches 扇出);失败短路不耗后续 RNG */
function applyChain(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, steps: ChainStep[], i: number, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  if (i >= steps.length) return true;
  const s = steps[i];
  switch (s.k) {
    case 'dither':
      return rng.next() >= s.p ? applyChain(st, rng, gs, steps, i + 1, x, y) : false;   // C1
    case 'blotches': {
      rng.next();                                   // 丢弃 1 掷(Modifiers.cs:130)
      if (rng.next() < s.chance) {                  // 成斑判定
        const x0 = rng.int(1 - s.minX, 0);          // Next(1-minX, 1)
        const x1 = rng.int(0, s.maxX - 1);          // Next(0, maxX)
        const y0 = rng.int(1 - s.minY, 0);
        const y1 = rng.int(0, s.maxY - 1);
        let ok = true;
        for (let dx = x0; dx <= x1; dx++)
          for (let dy = y0; dy <= y1; dy++)
            if (!applyChain(st, rng, gs, steps, i + 1, x + dx, y + dy)) ok = false;
        return ok;
      }
      return applyChain(st, rng, gs, steps, i + 1, x, y);
    }
    ...
  }
}
```

Wait, careful with `rng.int(0, s.maxX - 1)`: C# `Next(0, _maxX)` = [0, maxX-1] → rng.int(0, maxX-1) ✓. And `Next(1 - _minX, 1)` = [1-minX, 0] → rng.int(1-minX, 0) ✓.

Gen iteration helper:
```ts
/** WorldUtils.Gen + Shapes.Rectangle:列优先(x 外层/y 内层,Shapes.cs Rectangle.Perform) */
function genRect(st, rng, gs, steps, ox, oy, w, h) {
  for (let x = ox; x < ox + w; x++)
    for (let y = oy; y < oy + h; y++)
      applyChain(st, rng, gs, steps, 0, x, y);
}
```

Then each theme's AgeRoom becomes a sequence of genRect calls with the exact chains.

Now the actions:
- onlyTiles: `if (!activeAt) fail; if type ∈ types → next else fail`.
- onlyWalls: `if wall ∈ types → next else fail`.
- skipTiles: `if (!activeAt) → next; if type ∈ types → fail; else next`.
- isEmpty: `!activeAt(x,y) → next else fail`.
- offset: `applyChain(..., i+1, x+dx, y+dy)`.
- setTileKeepWall: `st.setTileSilent(x, y, tile)` — hmm, SetTileKeepWall clears the tile but KEEPS the wall; setTileSilent doesn't touch walls ✓. But it also sets frames via WorldUtils.TileFrame when setSelfFrames — our engine frames at render. Fine.

- stalagtite: placeTight (2 rolls + ice branch).
- vines: ActionVines.

	public class ClearMetadata : GenAction
	{
		public override bool Apply(Point origin, int x, int y, params object[] args)
		{
			GenBase._tiles[x, y].ClearMetadata();
			return UnitApply(origin, x, y, args);
		}
	}

	public class Clear : GenAction
	{
		public override bool Apply(Point origin, int x, int y, params object[] args)
		{
			GenBase._tiles[x, y].ClearEverything();
			return UnitApply(origin, x, y, args);
		}
	}

	public class ClearTile : GenAction
	{
		private bool _frameNeighbors;

		public ClearTile(bool frameNeighbors = false)
		{
			_frameNeighbors = frameNeighbors;
		}

		public override bool Apply(Point origin, int x, int y, params object[] args)
		{
			WorldUtils.ClearTile(x, y, _frameNeighbors);
			return UnitApply(origin, x, y, args);
		}
	}

	public class ClearWall : GenAction
	{
		private bool _frameNeighbors;

		public ClearWall(bool frameNeighbors = false)
		{
			_frameNeighbors = frameNeighbors;
		}

		public override bool Apply(Point origin, int x, int y, params object[] args)
		{
			WorldUtils.ClearWall(x, y, _frameNeighbors);
			return UnitApply(origin, x, y, args);
		}
	}

	public class HalfBlock : GenAction
	{
		private bool _value;

		public HalfBlock(bool value = true)
		{
			_value = value;
		}

		public override bool Apply(Point origin, int x, int y, params object[] args)
		{
			GenBase._tiles[x, y].halfBrick(_value);
			return UnitApply(origin, x, y, args);
		}
	}

	public class PlaceWall : GenAction
	{
		private ushort _type;

		private bool _neighbors;

		public PlaceWall(ushort type, bool neighbors = true)
		{
			_type = type;
			_neighbors = neighbors;
		}

		public override bool Apply(Point origin, int x, int y, params object[] args)
		{
			GenBase._tiles[x, y].wall = _type;
			WorldGen.SquareWallFrame(x, y);
			if (_neighbors)
			{
				WorldGen.SquareWallFrame(x + 1, y);
				WorldGen.SquareWallFrame(x - 1, y);
				WorldGen.SquareWallFrame(x, y - 1);
				WorldGen.SquareWallFrame(x, y + 1);
			}
			return UnitApply(origin, x, y, args);
		}
	}

	public class SetLiquid : GenAction
	{
		private int _type;

		private byte _value;

		public SetLiquid(int type = 0, byte value = byte.MaxValue)
		{
			_value = value;
			_type = type;
		}

		public override bool Apply(Point origin, int x, int y, params object[] args)
		{
			GenBase._tiles[x, y].liquidType(_type);
			GenBase._tiles[x, y].liquid = _value;
			return UnitApply(origin, x, y, args);
		}
	}

	public class SwapSolidTile : GenAction
	{
		private ushort _type;

		public SwapSolidTile(ushort type)
		{
			_type = type;
		}

		public override bool Apply(Point origin, int x, int y, params object[] args)
		{
			Tile tile = GenBase._tiles[x, y];
			if (WorldGen.SolidTile(tile))
			{
				tile.ResetToType(_type);
				return UnitApply(origin, x, y, args);
			}
			return Fail();
		}
	}

Now C7 implementation:

```ts
/** 三级级联放置(PlaceBiomeSpecificPriorityTool/PlaceBiomeSpecificTool 共形,
 *  HouseBuilder.cs:764-826/838-865/881-907):每房底行 10 次随机 → 失败整行扫描 →
 *  (仅 Bast)每房顶行 10 次随机 → 整行扫描。任一命中即停。 */
function cascadePlace(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, rooms: Rect[], key: string, ceiling: boolean): boolean {
  const tryAt = (r: Rect, y: number): boolean => {
    for (let t = 0; t < 10; t++) {
      if (placeFurniture(st, key, rng.int(2, r.w - 3) + r.x, y)) return true;
    }
    for (let x = r.x + 2; x <= r.x + r.w - 2; x++) {
      if (placeFurniture(st, key, x, y)) return true;
    }
    return false;
  };
  for (const r of rooms) if (tryAt(r, r.y + r.h - 2)) return true;
  if (ceiling) {
    for (const r of rooms) if (tryAt(r, r.y - 1)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}
```

 "old_string": "// ---------- Blotches 相干噪声(Modifiers.Blotches 近似:2 格团簇值噪声) ----------\nfunction makeBlotch(seed: number): (x: number, y: number) => number {\n  const hash = (x: number, y: number) => {\n    let h = (x * 374761393 + y * 668265263 + seed * 1442695041) | 0;\n    h = Math.imul(h ^ (h >>> 13), 1274126177);\n    return ((h ^ (h >>> 16)) >>> 0) / 4294967295;\n  };\n  return (x: number, y: number) => {\n    const gx = Math.floor(x / 2), gy = Math.floor(y / 2);\n    const fx = x / 2 - gx, fy = y / 2 - gy;\n    const sx = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx), sy = fy * fy * (3 - 2 * fy);\n    const a = hash(gx, gy), b = hash(gx + 1, gy), c = hash(gx, gy + 1), d = hash(gx + 1, gy + 1);\n    return a + (b - a) * sx + (c - a) * sy + (a - b - c + d) * sx * sy;\n  };\n}",
 "new_string": "// ---------- 原版 GenAction 链 1:1 解释器(Modifiers.cs / Actions.cs 逐条核实) ----------\n// 三条权威语义(旧实现全部踩错,修复根因):\n//  · Dither(p)(Modifiers.cs:621-635):p 是【失败率】——NextDouble() >= p 才放行,\n//    即 Dither(0.85) 通过率仅 15%。旧实现把 0.85 当通过率(< 0.85 即过),七主题\n//    蚀变量级整体取反(C1)。\n//  · Blotches(Modifiers.cs:92-152,Apply :131-152):每点【先丢 1 掷】,再\n//    NextDouble() < chance 决定是否成斑;成斑再掷 4 次(Next(1-minX,1)/Next(0,maxX)/\n//    Next(1-minY,1)/Next(0,maxY))并把后续链应用到随机矩形(会外溢房外);未成斑\n//    则原点继续。旧实现用自制哈希值噪声替斑,分布与掷骰数都不对(C2)。\n//  · 链序 = 掷骰序:Dither/Blotches 永远排在 OnlyTiles/OnlyWalls 之前\n//    (各 *HouseBuilder.cs AgeRoom),对房间矩形内每格都掷,失败短路后不耗 RNG(C3);\n//    墙动作的 worldSurface 二选一按【房间】room.Y 判定(WoodHouseBuilder.cs:36 /\n//    IceHouseBuilder.cs:32),非逐格。\ntype ChainStep =\n  | { k: 'dither'; p: number }                                            // Modifiers.Dither\n  | { k: 'blotches'; minX: number; maxX: number; minY: number; maxY: number; chance: number } // Modifiers.Blotches\n  | { k: 'onlyTiles'; types: number[] }                                   // Modifiers.OnlyTiles(Modifiers.cs:238)\n  | { k: 'onlyWalls'; types: number[] }                                   // Modifiers.OnlyWalls(Modifiers.cs:216)\n  | { k: 'skipTiles'; types: number[] }                                   // Modifiers.SkipTiles(Modifiers.cs:392)\n  | { k: 'isEmpty' }                                                      // Modifiers.IsEmpty(Modifiers.cs:539)\n  | { k: 'offset'; dx: number; dy: number }                               // Modifiers.Offset(Modifiers.cs:603)\n  | { k: 'setTileKeepWall'; tile: number }                                // Actions.SetTileKeepWall(Actions.cs:272)\n  | { k: 'setTile'; tile: number }                                        // Actions.SetTile(Actions.cs:203)\n  | { k: 'clearTile' }                                                    // Actions.ClearTile(Actions.cs:155)\n  | { k: 'clearWall' }                                                    // Actions.ClearWall(Actions.cs:171)\n  | { k: 'placeWall'; wall: number }                                      // Actions.PlaceWall(Actions.cs:516)\n  | { k: 'stalagtite' }                                                   // ActionStalagtite → WorldGen.PlaceTight\n  | { k: 'vines'; min: number; max: number; vine: number };               // ActionVines\n\n/** GenAction.Apply 第 i 步 @ (x,y),失败短路返回 false(不执行后续步骤、不耗 RNG) */\nfunction applyChain(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, steps: ChainStep[], i: number, x: number, y: number): boolean {\n  if (i >= steps.length) return true;\n  const s = steps[i];\n  switch (s.k) {\n    case 'dither':\n      // Modifiers.cs:630-635:NextDouble() >= _failureChance 才 UnitApply\n      return rng.next() >= s.p && applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x, y);\n    case 'blotches': {\n      rng.next();                                                    // 丢 1 掷(Modifiers.cs:132)\n      if (rng.next() < s.chance) {                                   // 成斑判定(:133)\n        const x0 = rng.int(1 - s.minX, 0);                           // Next(1-minX, 1)(:136)\n        const x1 = rng.int(0, s.maxX - 1);                           // Next(0, maxX)(:137)\n        const y0 = rng.int(1 - s.minY, 0);                           // Next(1-minY, 1)(:138)\n        const y1 = rng.int(0, s.maxY - 1);                           // Next(0, maxY)(:139)\n        let ok = true;                                               // flag |= !UnitApply(:140-145)\n        for (let dx = x0; dx <= x1; dx++) {\n          for (let dy = y0; dy <= y1; dy++) {\n            if (!applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x + dx, y + dy)) ok = false;\n          }\n        }\n        return ok;\n      }\n      return applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x, y);                // 未成斑原点继续(:148)\n    }\n    case 'onlyTiles': {\n      if (!activeAt(st, x, y)) return false;                         // !active → Fail(:245)\n      const t = st.type[st.idx(x, y)];\n      return s.types.includes(t) && applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x, y);\n    }\n    case 'onlyWalls':\n      return s.types.includes(st.wall[st.idx(x, y)]) && applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x, y);\n    case 'skipTiles': {\n      if (!activeAt(st, x, y)) return applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x, y); // !active 放行(:399)\n      return !s.types.includes(st.type[st.idx(x, y)]) && applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x, y);\n    }\n    case 'isEmpty':\n      return !activeAt(st, x, y) && applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x, y);\n    case 'offset':\n      return applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x + s.dx, y + s.dy);\n    case 'setTileKeepWall':\n      // Actions.cs:272-301:清块保墙(墙由本仓墙段独立处理)+ type + active,恒成功\n      st.setTileSilent(x, y, s.tile);\n      return applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x, y);\n    case 'setTile':\n      st.setTileSilent(x, y, s.tile);\n      return applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x, y);\n    case 'clearTile':\n      st.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);\n      return applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x, y);\n    case 'clearWall':\n      st.setWallSilent(x, y, 0);\n      return applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x, y);\n    case 'placeWall':\n      st.setWallSilent(x, y, s.wall);\n      return applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x, y);\n    case 'stalagtite':\n      placeTight(st, rng, x, y);                                     // ActionStalagtite 恒 UnitApply\n      return applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x, y);\n    case 'vines': {\n      // ActionVines.cs:21-35:len=Next(min,max+1);逐格向下铺到实心;i>0 才算成功\n      const len = rng.int(s.min, s.max);\n      let placed = 0;\n      for (let k = 0; k < len && st.inBounds(x, y + k) && !activeAt(st, x, y + k); k++) {\n        st.setTileSilent(x, y + k, s.vine, 0, 0);\n        placed++;\n      }\n      return placed > 0 && applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x, y);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/** WorldUtils.Gen + Shapes.Rectangle(Shapes.cs Rectangle.Perform):x 外层 / y 内层 */\nfunction genRect(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, steps: ChainStep[], ox: number, oy: number, w: number, h: number): void {\n  for (let x = ox; x < ox + w; x++) {\n    for (let y = oy; y < oy + h; y++) applyChain(st, rng, steps, 0, x, y);\n  }\n}"
}

 "file_path": "~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/world/gen/vanilla/CaveHousePass.ts",
 "old_string": "// ---------- AgeRoom 七主题蚀变 ----------\n/** PlaceTight 冰系钟乳石(PlaceUncheckedStalactite 冰分支:上方∈{147,161,163,164,200}) */\nfunction placeStalactiteIce(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number): void {\n  if (!solidAt(st, x, y - 1) || activeAt(st, x, y) || activeAt(st, x, y + 1)) return;\n  const above = st.type[st.idx(x, y - 1)];\n  if (above !== SNOW147 && above !== ICE161\n    && above !== TILE_BY_KEY['v_163_corrupt_ice_block']! && above !== sheetTile(164)\n    && above !== TILE_BY_KEY['v_200_crimson_ice_block']!) return;\n  const preferSmall = rng.int(0, 1) === 0;\n  const variation = rng.int(0, 2);\n  // 冰族专属列（GetDesiredStalagtiteStyle :38800-38808 重定型）：147/161→0、\n  // 163→594、164→540、200→648——曾全写 fx0 冰柱列（163/164/200 错列）\n  const base = above === SNOW147 || above === ICE161 ? 0\n    : above === TILE_BY_KEY['v_163_corrupt_ice_block']! ? 594\n    : above === sheetTile(164) ? 540 : 648;\n  if (preferSmall) {\n    st.setTileSilent(x, y, STALACTITE, base + variation * 18, 72);\n  } else {\n    st.setTileSilent(x, y, STALACTITE, base + variation * 18, 0);\n    st.setTileSilent(x, y + 1, STALACTITE, base + variation * 18, 18);\n  }\n}\n/** 花岗岩/大理石/沙漠钟乳石:原版 PlaceUncheckedStalactite 的冰/石双名单不含\n *  368/367/397 → 恒 no-op(原版语义,cs:38378 名单外静默返回) */\nfunction placeStalactiteNoop(): void { /* 原版名单外无操作 */ }",
 "new_string": "// ---------- 钟乳石(WorldGen.PlaceTight 1:1) ----------\n/** PlaceUncheckedStalactite 的冰分支(cs:38377-38405):上方 ∈{147,161,163,164,200}\n *  才放置;其余名单(石/苔/沙等)在地下屋场景上方是主题块,恒静默返回 */\nfunction placeUncheckedStalactite(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, preferSmall: boolean, variation: number): void {\n  if (!solidAt(st, x, y - 1) || activeAt(st, x, y) || activeAt(st, x, y + 1)) return;\n  const above = st.type[st.idx(x, y - 1)];\n  if (above !== SNOW147 && above !== ICE161\n    && above !== TILE_BY_KEY['v_163_corrupt_ice_block']! && above !== sheetTile(164)\n    && above !== TILE_BY_KEY['v_200_crimson_ice_block']!) return;\n  // 冰族专属列（GetDesiredStalagtiteStyle :38800-38808 重定型）：147/161→0、\n  // 163→594、164→540、200→648——曾全写 fx0 冰柱列（163/164/200 错列）\n  const base = above === SNOW147 || above === ICE161 ? 0\n    : above === TILE_BY_KEY['v_163_corrupt_ice_block']! ? 594\n    : above === sheetTile(164) ? 540 : 648;\n  if (preferSmall) {\n    st.setTileSilent(x, y, STALACTITE, base + variation * 18, 72);\n  } else {\n    st.setTileSilent(x, y, STALACTITE, base + variation * 18, 0);\n    st.setTileSilent(x, y + 1, STALACTITE, base + variation * 18, 18);\n  }\n}\n\n/** WorldGen.PlaceTight(cs:38329-38351):231 幼虫(及 shimmer,生成期无来源)短路,\n *  其余【恒先掷 2 次】Next(2)/Next(3) 再走 PlaceUncheckedStalactite——名单外静默\n *  返回但掷骰照耗(沙漠/花岗岩/大理石的 ActionStalagtite 行因此也要走这里) */\nfunction placeTight(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number): void {\n  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;\n  const i = st.idx(x, y);\n  if (st.flags[i] !== 0 && st.type[i] === LARVA231) return;\n  const preferSmall = rng.int(0, 1) === 0;\n  const variation = rng.int(0, 2);\n  placeUncheckedStalactite(st, x, y, preferSmall, variation);\n}"
}

x < r.x + r.w; x++) {\n      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {\n        if (st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.tile || !activeAt(st, x, y)) continue;\n        if (rng.next() < 0.6 && blob(x, y) < 0.6) {\n          st.setTileSilent(x, y, JUNGLE60);\n          if (rng.next() < 0.8) st.setTileSilent(x, y, MUD59);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    // 60 行垂藤(ActionVines(3, room.Height, 62):长 3..H,遇实心止)\n    for (const rowY of [r.y, r.y + r.h - 1]) {\n      for (let x = r.x + 1; x < r.x + r.w - 1; x++) {\n        if (activeAt(st, x, rowY) && st.type[st.idx(x, rowY)] === JUNGLE60 && rng.next() < 0.5) {\n          const len = rng.int(3, r.h);\n          for (let i = 0; i < len && !activeAt(st, x, rowY + 1 + i); i++) {\n            st.setTileSilent(x, rowY + 1 + i, VINE62, 0, 0);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    // 墙 → 64(无 OnlyWalls)\n    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {\n      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {\n        if (rng.next() < 0.85 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) st.setWallSilent(x, y, 64);\n      }\n    }\n    return;\n  }\n  if (theme === 'mushroom') {\n    // 190 →(0.7+团簇0.5)→ 70\n    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {\n      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {\n        if (st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.tile || !activeAt(st, x, y)) continue;\n        if (rng.next() < 0.7 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) st.setTileSilent(x, y, MUSH70);\n      }\n    }\n    // 70 行上方发光菇 71(Offset(0,-1),IsEmpty)\n    for (const rowY of [r.y, r.y + r.h - 1]) {\n      for (let x = r.x + 1; x < r.x + r.w - 1; x++) {\n        if (activeAt(st, x, rowY) && st.type[st.idx(x, rowY)] === MUSH70 && rng.next() < 0.6) {\n          if (!activeAt(st, x, rowY - 1)) st.setTileSilent(x, rowY - 1, SHROOM71, 0, 0);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    // 墙清空(无 OnlyWalls)\n    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {\n      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {\n        if (rng.next() < 0.85 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) st.setWallSilent(x, y, 0);\n      }\n    }\n    return;\n  }\n  if (theme === 'granite' || theme === 'marble') {\n    // 369→368 / 357→367(0.6+团簇);钟乳石 = 原版名单外 no-op;墙 → 180/178\n    const to = theme === 'granite' ? GRANITE368 : MARBLE367;\n    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {\n      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {\n        if (st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.tile || !activeAt(st, x, y)) continue;\n        if (rng.next() < 0.6 && blob(x, y) < 0.6) st.setTileSilent(x, y, to);\n      }\n    }\n    for (const rowY of [r.y, r.y + r.h - 1]) {\n      for (let x = r.x + 1; x < r.x + r.w - 1; x++) {\n        const t = st.type[st.idx(x, rowY)];\n        if (activeAt(st, x, rowY) && t === to && rng.next() < 0.8) placeStalactiteNoop();\n      }\n    }\n    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {\n      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {\n        if (rng.next() < 0.85 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) st.setWallSilent(x, y, theme === 'granite' ? 180 : 178);\n      }\n    }\n    return;\n  }\n  // desert:396 →(0.8+团簇0.2)→ 397;钟乳石名单外 no-op;OnlyWalls(187) → 墙 216\n  for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {\n    for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {\n      if (st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.tile || !activeAt(st, x, y)) continue;\n      if (rng.next() < 0.8 && blob(x, y) < 0.2 && rng.next() < 0.5) st.setTileSilent(x, y, HARDSAND397);\n    }\n  }\n  for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {\n    for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {\n      if (st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.wall) continue;\n      if (rng.next() < 0.8 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) st.setWallSilent(x, y, 216);\n    }\n  }\n}",
 "new_string": "/** AgeRoom 七主题蚀变:逐主题把原版 WorldUtils.Gen 链逐条转录(行号 = 各\n *  *HouseBuilder.cs AgeRoom)。Dither/Blotches 语义见 applyChain 头注(C1/C2/C3) */\nfunction ageRoom(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, r: Rect, theme: Theme, def: ThemeDef): void {\n  // Blotches(scale=2)→ minX=maxX=minY=maxY=2(Modifiers.cs:95-100)\n  const B2 = (chance: number): ChainStep => ({ k: 'blotches', minX: 2, maxX: 2, minY: 2, maxY: 2, chance });\n  // 墙段收尾:room.Y > worldSurface 时清墙、否则铺墙——按【房间】整体二选一\n  // (WoodHouseBuilder.cs:36 / IceHouseBuilder.cs:32 的三元表达式在链构造时求值)\n  const wallTail = (surfaceWall: number): ChainStep[] => [\n    { k: 'skipTiles', types: SKIP_AGING_TILES },\n    r.y > gs.worldSurface ? { k: 'clearWall' } : { k: 'placeWall', wall: surfaceWall },\n  ];\n\n  if (theme === 'wood') {\n    // ① 蛛网(WoodHouseBuilder.cs:30-35):W*H/16 次,每处 2×2 矩形跑\n    //   Dither(0.5)+Blotches(2,2,chance 0.3)+IsEmpty+SetTile(51)\n    const count = Math.floor(r.w * r.h / 16);\n    for (let c = 0; c < count; c++) {\n      const x = rng.int(1, r.w - 2) + r.x;\n      const y = rng.int(1, r.h - 2) + r.y;\n      genRect(st, rng, [{ k: 'dither', p: 0.5 }, B2(0.3), { k: 'isEmpty' }, { k: 'setTile', tile: COBWEB }], x, y, 2, 2);\n    }\n    // ② 墙侵蚀(WoodHouseBuilder.cs:36):Dither(0.85)+Blotches()+OnlyWalls(27)+\n    //   SkipTiles(画族)+清墙/铺土墙 2\n    genRect(st, rng, [\n      { k: 'dither', p: 0.85 }, B2(0.3), { k: 'onlyWalls', types: [def.wall] }, ...wallTail(2),\n    ], r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h);\n    // ③ 第三段(WoodHouseBuilder.cs:37,曾整体缺失 C4):Dither(0.95)+\n    //    OnlyTiles(30,321,158)+ClearTile——剥掉木/针叶/红木主题块\n    genRect(st, rng, [\n      { k: 'dither', p: 0.95 },\n      { k: 'onlyTiles', types: [T.WOOD, TILE_BY_KEY['v_321_boreal_wood']!, TILE_BY_KEY['v_158_rich_mahogany']!] },\n      { k: 'clearTile' },\n    ], r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h);\n    return;\n  }\n  if (theme === 'ice') {\n    // ① 321→161→147(IceHouseBuilder.cs:29):Dither(0.6)+Blotches(2,0.6)+\n    //   OnlyTiles(321)+SetTileKeepWall(161)+Dither(0.8)+SetTileKeepWall(147)\n    genRect(st, rng, [\n      { k: 'dither', p: 0.6 }, B2(0.6), { k: 'onlyTiles', types: [def.tile] },\n      { k: 'setTileKeepWall', tile: ICE161 },\n      { k: 'dither', p: 0.8 }, { k: 'setTileKeepWall', tile: SNOW147 },\n    ], r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h);\n    // ② 顶/底行钟乳石(IceHouseBuilder.cs:30-31):Dither(0.5)+OnlyTiles(161)+\n    //   Offset(0,1)+ActionStalagtite,矩形 (X+1, 行, W-2, 1)\n    for (const rowY of [r.y, r.y + r.h - 1]) {\n      genRect(st, rng, [\n        { k: 'dither', p: 0.5 }, { k: 'onlyTiles', types: [ICE161] }, { k: 'offset', dx: 0, dy: 1 }, { k: 'stalagtite' },\n      ], r.x + 1, rowY, r.w - 2, 1);\n    }\n    // ③ 墙(IceHouseBuilder.cs:32,无 OnlyWalls):Dither(0.85)+Blotches(2,0.8)+\n    //   SkipTiles(画族)+清墙/铺墙 40\n    genRect(st, rng, [{ k: 'dither', p: 0.85 }, B2(0.8), ...wallTail(40)], r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h);\n    return;\n  }\n  if (theme === 'jungle') {\n    // ① 158→60→59(JungleHouseBuilder.cs:29):Dither(0.6)+Blotches(2,0.6)+\n    //   OnlyTiles(158)+SetTileKeepWall(60)+Dither(0.8)+SetTileKeepWall(59)\n    genRect(st, rng, [\n      { k: 'dither', p: 0.6 }, B2(0.6), { k: 'onlyTiles', types: [def.tile] },\n      { k: 'setTileKeepWall', tile: JUNGLE60 },\n      { k: 'dither', p: 0.8 }, { k: 'setTileKeepWall', tile: MUD59 },\n    ], r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h);\n    // ② 藤(JungleHouseBuilder.cs:30-31):Dither(0.5)+OnlyTiles(60)+Offset(0,1)+\n    //   IsEmpty+ActionVines(3, H, 62)\n    for (const rowY of [r.y, r.y + r.h - 1]) {\n      genRect(st, rng, [\n        { k: 'dither', p: 0.5 }, { k: 'onlyTiles', types: [JUNGLE60] }, { k: 'offset', dx: 0, dy: 1 },\n        { k: 'isEmpty' }, { k: 'vines', min: 3, max: r.h, vine: VINE62 },\n      ], r.x + 1, rowY, r.w - 2, 1);\n    }\n    // ③ 墙 64(JungleHouseBuilder.cs:32,无 OnlyWalls/SkipTiles)\n    genRect(st, rng, [{ k: 'dither', p: 0.85 }, B2(0.3), { k: 'placeWall', wall: 64 }], r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h);\n    return;\n  }\n  if (theme === 'mushroom') {\n    // ① 190→70(MushroomHouseBuilder.cs:27):Dither(0.7)+Blotches(2,0.5)+OnlyTiles(190)\n    genRect(st, rng, [\n      { k: 'dither', p: 0.7 }, B2(0.5), { k: 'onlyTiles', types: [def.tile] },\n      { k: 'setTileKeepWall', tile: MUSH70 },\n    ], r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h);\n    // ② 顶/底行(MushroomHouseBuilder.cs:28-29):Dither(0.6)+OnlyTiles(70)+\n    //   Offset(0,-1)+IsEmpty+SetTile(71)\n    for (const rowY of [r.y, r.y + r.h - 1]) {\n      genRect(st, rng, [\n        { k: 'dither', p: 0.6 }, { k: 'onlyTiles', types: [MUSH70] }, { k: 'offset', dx: 0, dy: -1 },\n        { k: 'isEmpty' }, { k: 'setTile', tile: SHROOM71 },\n      ], r.x + 1, rowY, r.w - 2, 1);\n    }\n    // ③ 墙清空(MushroomHouseBuilder.cs:30)\n    genRect(st, rng, [{ k: 'dither', p: 0.85 }, B2(0.3), { k: 'clearWall' }], r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h);\n    return;\n  }\n  if (theme === 'granite' || theme === 'marble') {\n    // ① 369→368 / 357→367(GraniteHouseBuilder.cs:28 / MarbleHouseBuilder.cs:28):\n    //   Dither(0.6)+Blotches(2,0.6)+OnlyTiles(主题块)+SetTileKeepWall(368/367)\n    const to = theme === 'granite' ? GRANITE368 : MARBLE367;\n    genRect(st, rng, [\n      { k: 'dither', p: 0.6 }, B2(0.6), { k: 'onlyTiles', types: [def.tile] },\n      { k: 'setTileKeepWall', tile: to },\n    ], r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h);\n    // ② 顶/底行(GraniteHouseBuilder.cs:29-30):Dither(0.8)+OnlyTiles(368/367)+\n    //   Offset(0,1)+ActionStalagtite(名单外 no-op,但 Dither/PlaceTight 掷骰照耗)\n    for (const rowY of [r.y, r.y + r.h - 1]) {\n      genRect(st, rng, [\n        { k: 'dither', p: 0.8 }, { k: 'onlyTiles', types: [to] }, { k: 'offset', dx: 0, dy: 1 }, { k: 'stalagtite' },\n      ], r.x + 1, rowY, r.w - 2, 1);\n    }\n    // ③ 墙 180/178(GraniteHouseBuilder.cs:31 / MarbleHouseBuilder.cs:31)\n    genRect(st, rng, [\n      { k: 'dither', p: 0.85 }, B2(0.3), { k: 'placeWall', wall: theme === 'granite' ? 180 : 178 },\n    ], r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h);\n    return;\n  }\n  // desert\n  // ① 396→396→397(DesertHouseBuilder.cs:31):Dither(0.8)+Blotches(2,0.2)+\n  //   OnlyTiles(396)+SetTileKeepWall(396)+Dither(0.5)+SetTileKeepWall(397)\n  //   ——第一段写 396 是原版字面(视觉 no-op),但第二道 Dither 的掷骰位置在此\n  genRect(st, rng, [\n    { k: 'dither', p: 0.8 }, B2(0.2), { k: 'onlyTiles', types: [def.tile] },\n    { k: 'setTileKeepWall', tile: def.tile },\n    { k: 'dither', p: 0.5 }, { k: 'setTileKeepWall', tile: HARDSAND397 },\n  ], r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h);\n  // ② 顶/底行钟乳石(DesertHouseBuilder.cs:32-33,曾整体缺失 C5):\n  //   Dither(0.5)+OnlyTiles(397,396)+Offset(0,1)+ActionStalagtite\n  for (const rowY of [r.y, r.y + r.h - 1]) {\n    genRect(st, rng, [\n      { k: 'dither', p: 0.5 }, { k: 'onlyTiles', types: [HARDSAND397, def.tile] },\n      { k: 'offset', dx: 0, dy: 1 }, { k: 'stalagtite' },\n    ], r.x + 1, rowY, r.w - 2, 1);\n  }\n  // ③ 墙 216(DesertHouseBuilder.cs:34):Dither(0.8)+Blotches()+OnlyWalls(187)\n  genRect(st, rng, [\n    { k: 'dither', p: 0.8 }, B2(0.3), { k: 'onlyWalls', types: [def.wall] }, { k: 'placeWall', wall: 216 },\n  ], r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h);\n}"
}

 "new_string": "// ---------- 主题专属件级联(PlaceBiomeSpecificPriorityTool / PlaceBiomeSpecificTool) ----------\n/** HouseBuilder.cs:764-826/838-865/881-907 共形级联:逐房【底行 Y+H-2】先 10 次\n *  随机(Next(2,W-2)+X)再整行扫描;仅 Bast 有顶行 Y-1 兜底级联(:798-825)。\n *  任一命中即停(true = 放置成功,调用方据此递增计数)。\n *  旧实现是\"rooms[0] 单发随机公式\"——三级级联与逐房推进全部缺失(C7) */\nfunction cascadePlace(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, rooms: Rect[], key: string, ceilingFallback: boolean): boolean {\n  const tryRow = (r: Rect, y: number): boolean => {\n    for (let t = 0; t < 10; t++) {\n      if (placeFurniture(st, key, rng.int(2, r.w - 3) + r.x, y)) return true;\n    }\n    for (let x = r.x + 2; x <= r.x + r.w - 2; x++) {\n      if (placeFurniture(st, key, x, y)) return true;\n    }\n    return false;\n  };\n  for (const r of rooms) if (tryRow(r, r.y + r.h - 2)) return true;\n  if (ceilingFallback) {\n    for (const r of rooms) if (tryRow(r, r.y - 1)) return true;\n  }\n  return false;\n}\n\n// ---------- 宝箱四级联(PlaceChests) ----------"
}

 "new_string": "// ---------- pass 主体 ----------\n/** 地下小屋 pass(UndergroundHousesAndBuriedChests 的 CaveHouse 段,cs:17153-17192\n *  + 沙漠追加段 cs:17193-17219) */\nexport function runCaveHousesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {\n  const { w, h } = st;\n  // CaveHouseCount 35-40 × WorldArea/5040000(Configuration.json)。WorldGenRange.\n  // GetRandom(WorldGenRange.cs:34-40)= Next(ScaledMinimum, ScaledMaximum+1),\n  // ScaleValue(:42-57)先按面积缩放边界再 (int) 截断——即\n  // Next((int)(35*s), (int)(40*s)+1)。旧实现\"先掷后乘 + Math.round\",分布不同(C10)。\n  // 另:原版 pass 头连掷四枚(cs:17081-17084:CaveHouseCount→UnderworldChestCount→\n  // CaveChestCount→AdditionalDesertHouseCount);本仓拆分后 UnderworldChestCount/\n  // CaveChestCount 归 BuriedChestsPass 侧掷(runBuriedChestsPass/runUnderworldChestsPass,\n  // WorldGen.ts:711/:785),此处只掷本 pass 拥有的两枚,勿在此重复掷。\n  const s = (w * h) / 5040000;\n  const count = rng.int(Math.trunc(35 * s), Math.trunc(40 * s));\n  // 第 4 个 WorldGenRange：AdditionalDesertHouseCount（2-2 × WorldArea，\n  // Configuration.json）——GetRandom 恒消费一次 Next（cs:17084）\n  const desertHouses = rng.int(2, 2);\n  let bastCount = 0;        // GenVars.extraBastStatueCount\n  // extraBastStatueCountMax(WorldGen.cs:11490-11499)= 2+尺寸档:大(≥8400)+2、\n  // 中(≥6400)+1、小 +0——旧实现硬编码 2,仅小世界正确(C7)\n  const bastMax = 2 + (w >= 8400 ? 2 : w >= 6400 ? 1 : 0);\n  let extractCount = 0;     // HouseBuilderContext.ExtractinatorCount\n  let sharpCount = 0;       // context.SharpenerCount\n\n  /** 单座小屋放置（HouseBuilder.Place 原序），返回是否成功 */\n  const placeHouse = (ox: number, oy: number, oceanGate: boolean): boolean => {\n    if (oceanGate && (ox < gs.beachDistance || ox > w - gs.beachDistance)) return false; // oceanDepths 回避\n    // CaveHouseBiome.Place 前置拒绝(CaveHouseBiome.cs:36-54,C9 曾整体缺失):\n    //   InWorld(origin,30) + 原点 ±25 内有红线或 BasicChest(21/467,TileID.cs:315)\n    //   → 整屋放弃。须在 createRooms 之前(不耗 RNG)。\n    if (ox < 30 || oy < 30 || ox >= w - 30 || oy >= h - 30) return false;\n    for (let i = ox - 25; i <= ox + 25; i++) {\n      for (let j = oy - 25; j <= oy + 25; j++) {\n        const k = st.idx(i, j);\n        if ((st.wire[k] & TileStore.WIRE_RED) !== 0) return false; // tile.wire()(红线位)\n        const t = st.type[k];\n        if (t === CHEST21 || t === CHEST2) return false;           // TileID.Sets.BasicChest\n      }\n    }\n    const rooms = createRooms(st, rng, ox, oy);"
}

Rooms are >= 10 from world edge (AreRoomLocationsValid with fluff 10), so ±1 is safe. But `activeAt` bounds-checks first ✓ (it's `st.inBounds(x, y) && ...`). However `st.type[st.idx(x, y)]` is only reached after activeAt passed → in bounds ✓. Still, for robustness let me add bounds guards to onlyWalls to avoid a potential OOB read. Actually `st.idx` for out-of-range values would compute a garbage index into the typed arrays — no crash in JS (returns undefined → includes(undefined) = false) but still sloppy. Rooms are guaranteed ≥10 in-bounds, so it can't trigger. I'll add a cheap guard anyway for onlyWalls to match the others' safety.

In the ICE theme's second segment, `genRect(..., r.x + 1, rowY, r.w - 2, 1)` then offset dy=+1 → stalagtite at rowY+1, then placeTight reads x,y-1 etc. For rowY = r.y + r.h - 1 (bottom row), rowY+1 = r.y + r.h → one row BELOW the room (the room's floor row is r.y+r.h-1... hmm actually the room rect's last row). placeTight at (x, r.y+r.h) — in bounds ✓ (rooms are ≥10 from edges and ≥220 from bottom).

262	function applyChain(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, steps: ChainStep[], i: number, x: number, y: number): boolean {

284	      return applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x, y);                // 未成斑原点继续(:148)

305	    case 'setTile':

308	    case 'clearTile':

310	      return applyChain(st, rng, steps, i + 1, x, y);

818	          }
819	        }
820	      }
821	      if (!ok) return false;
822	    }
823

825	    // 1) PlaceEmptyRooms:外壳 + 内腔墙

829	          st.setTileSilent(x, y, def.tile);
830	          st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] = 0;

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * 一次性诊断脚本的统一 runner(防孤儿进程)。
 *
 * 背景:2026-08-10 发现 7 个 `vite-node tools/_diag-gen.mjs` 孤儿进程,
 * 每个占满 1 核空转 4 小时(脚本 import 链带起不可退出的活句柄,TERM 信号都无法处理)。
 * 约定:以后所有 _diag-*.mjs 一律经本 runner 运行,不再直接 npm exec vite-node。
 *
 * 用法:
 *   node tools/run-diag.mjs [--timeout=ms] <script.mjs> [传给脚本的参数...]
 *
 * 双层架构:
 *   - 子进程(--_child,内部):导入目标脚本;导入完成 0.5s 后强制 exit(0),
 *     不依赖事件循环自然排空——兜住"脚本主体完成但残留 interval/server 等活句柄"(本次事故形态);
 *   - 父进程(默认):只等子进程退出 + 看门狗;子进程超时(默认 120s)未退 → SIGKILL,exit 2。
 *     父进程事件循环始终空闲,因此子进程无论是同步死循环、永不 resolve 的 await
 *     还是任何未知卡死,都必然在 timeout 内被强杀。
 *
 * 退出码:0 正常 / 1 脚本异常或被信号终止 / 2 超时强杀 / 64 用法错误,其余透传脚本自设退出码。
 */
const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 120_000;
const FLUSH_MS = 500;

const argv = process.argv.slice(2);

// ---------- 子进程模式:导入目标脚本 ----------
if (argv[0] === '--_child') {
  const target = argv[1];
  try {
    const { pathToFileURL } = await import('node:url');
    const path = await import('node:path');
    await import(pathToFileURL(path.resolve(process.cwd(), target)).href);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[run-diag] 脚本异常:', e);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  // 主体完成:给 stdout 冲刷留 0.5s,无视残留活句柄强制正常退出。
  setTimeout(() => process.exit(0), FLUSH_MS).unref?.();
} else {
  // ---------- 父进程模式:spawn 子进程 + 看门狗 ----------
  let timeoutMs = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS;
  const rest = [];
  for (const a of argv) {
    if (a.startsWith('--timeout=')) {
      const v = Number(a.slice('--timeout='.length));
      if (Number.isFinite(v) && v > 0) timeoutMs = v;
    } else {
      rest.push(a);
    }
  }

```

  const child = spawn(process.execPath, [self, '--_child', target, ...rest.slice(1)], {
    stdio: 'inherit',
  });

  let done = false;
  const watchdog = setTimeout(() => {
    if (done) return;
    done = true;
    console.error(`[run-diag] 超时 ${timeoutMs}ms,SIGKILL 子进程 (exit 2)`);
    child.kill('SIGKILL');
    process.exit(2);
  }, timeoutMs);

  child.on('exit', (code, signal) => {
    if (done) return;
    done = true;
    clearTimeout(watchdog);
    if (signal) {
      console.error(`[run-diag] 子进程被信号 ${signal} 终止 (exit 1)`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
    process.exit(code ?? 1);
  });

  child.on('error', (e) => {
    console.error('[run-diag] 启动失败:', e);
    process.exit(1);
  });

708	      if (placeFurniture(st, key, x, y)) return true;
709	    }
710	    return false;
711	  };
712	  for (const r of rooms) if (tryRow(r, r.y + r.h - 2)) return true;
713	  if (ceilingFallback) {
714	    for (const r of rooms) if (tryRow(r, r.y - 1)) return true;
715	  }
716	  return false;
717	}
718	
719	// ---------- 宝箱四级联(PlaceChests) ----------

## 修复报告(仅改 `game/src/world/gen/vanilla/CaveHousePass.ts`)

每条均先 sed 打开 1456 原版行核实后才动手；两处发现 audit 引文有误，已按核实结果改判并说明。

### C1-C10 处置

**C1 已修(引文准确)** — 核实 `Modifiers.cs:621-635`:`Dither` 参数确为失败率，`NextDouble() >= _failureChance` 才放行。新实现 `applyChain` 的 dither 分支统一 `rng.next() >= p`,七主题全部门(0.85/0.8/0.7/0.6/0.5)不再取反。

**C2 已修(引文准确)** — 核实 `Modifiers.cs:92-152`(Apply :131-152):先丢 1 掷、再 `NextDouble() < chance` 成斑、成斑掷 4 次(`Next(1-minX,1)`/`Next(0,maxX)`/同 Y)并扇出到随机矩形(可外溢房外)。自制 `makeBlotch` 哈希噪声整体删除，换成 `applyChain` 的 blotches 分支(扇出递归，`flag |= !UnitApply` 语义保留)。各主题实参逐一转录：Wood 蛛网 `Blotches(2,2)`=chance 0.3、各墙段 `Blotches()`=0.3、Ice/Jungle/Granite/Marble ①=0.6、Ice 墙段=0.8、Desert ①=0.2、Mushroom ①=0.5。

**C3 已修(核实后追加一处同根偏差)** — 七主题 AgeRoom 重写为逐段 `genRect`(x 外层/y 内层，同 `Shapes.Rectangle.Perform`),Dither/Blotches 严格排在 OnlyTiles/OnlyWalls 之前，房间矩形内每格都掷、失败短路不耗 RNG。核实时另发现：墙动作的 worldSurface 二选一是按**房间** `room.Y` 判定(WoodHouseBuilder.cs:36 / IceHouseBuilder.cs:32 的三元在链构造时求值)，旧实现逐格判 `y > worldSurface`——一并修正。

**C4 已修** — WoodHouseBuilder.cs:37 第三段补上:`Dither(0.95) → OnlyTiles(30,321,158) → ClearTile`(30=T.WOOD、321/158 取既有 key)。

**C5 已修** — DesertHouseBuilder.cs:32-33 两行补上:`Dither(0.5) → OnlyTiles(397,396) → Offset(0,1) → ActionStalagtite`。核实 `PlaceTight`(WorldGen.cs:38329-38351)发现它在名单外也**恒掷 2 次**(Next(2)/Next(3),仅 231 幼虫/shimmer 短路)，故把 `placeStalactiteIce` 重构为 `placeTight`(先掷后守卫)+ `placeUncheckedStalactite`(纯冰族放置)，沙漠/花岗岩/大理石行照耗掷骰。

**C6 已修，但 audit 轴向引文有误** — `HouseBuilder.cs:518-519/:574` 用 `PlaceTile(19, PlatformStyle)` 属实，但 `WorldGen.PlaceTile` case 19(WorldGen.cs:60245-60249)是 `tile.frameY = (short)(18 * style)`,**frameY 轴而非 audit 所说的 frameX**。已按核实结果写 `(0, platformStyle*18)`——与本仓 LivingTreePass/HellFortPass/DungeonPass 的既有约定一致(它们早已用 frameY)。七主题 PlatformStyle(0/19/2/42/28/29/18)与 THEMES 表核对无误。楼梯、顶步 4×1、竖井上下出口共 4 处全改。

**C7 已修(核实后追加一处)** — `HouseBuilder.cs:757-831/:833-913` 三/四级级联落成 `cascadePlace`(逐房底行 10 次随机→整行扫描；仅 Bast 有顶行 Y-1 兜底级联)，替换单发公式；磨刀站/提炼机上限改**每屋重掷** `rng.int(2,4)`(=Next(2,5),条件短路保证仅对应主题掷)。核实中另发现 `extraBastStatueCountMax = 2 + num13`(WorldGen.cs:11490-11499,大世界 +2/中 +1/小 +0),audit 的“≤2”只对小世界成立——已按尺寸档实现 `bastMax`。

**C8 已修** — WorldGen.cs:17152-17192 核实：失败(oceanDepths/Place 失败)`num5--; k--` 共享预算重掷。主循环改为 `fail=10000` 预算 + 失败不进位重试。

**C9 已修** — CaveHouseBiome.cs:36-54 核实:`InWorld(origin,30)` + 原点 ±25 内有 wire 或 BasicChest → 整屋放弃，且在 createRooms 之前(不耗 RNG)。wire 用 `TileStore.WIRE_RED`(bit0,= 原版 `tile.wire()` 红线位，本仓 placeStatueTrap 也只写 bit0);**BasicChest 精确取 {21,467}**(TileID.cs:315,用 `TILE_BY_KEY['chest']` 与既有 CHEST2 常量)——比任务建议的 MicroBiomesPass IsAContainer 口径更窄更准(该口径含 dresser 88,不在 BasicChest 内)。

**C10 已修(掷骰归属已查明，未重复掷)** — WorldGenRange.cs:34-57 核实：先缩放边界再 `(int)` 截断，即 `Next((int)(35s), (int)(40s)+1)` → 改为 `rng.int(Math.trunc(35*s), Math.trunc(40*s))`,废弃“先掷后乘+Math.round”。四枚掷骰归属:**CaveChestCount 在 BuriedChestsPass.ts:499**(runBuriedChestsPass,WorldGen.ts:711 先于本 pass)、**UnderworldChestCount 在 BuriedChestsPass.ts:516**(runUnderworldChestsPass,WorldGen.ts:785 地狱段，有意拆分)——本文件只掷自己拥有的 CaveHouseCount + AdditionalDesertHouseCount 两枚。两点备案：①本仓掷骰序(CaveChest→CaveHouse→AdditionalDesert)与原版(CaveHouse→Underworld→CaveChest→AdditionalDesert,cs:17081-17084)不一致，是 pass 拆分的架构性结果，修它要动 BuriedChestsPass/WorldGen.ts(禁改，仅报告)；②BuriedChestsPass.ts:499 存在同款旧公式 bug(禁改，仅报告)。
